# LORAMER_DECISIONS.md — SETTLED-DECISIONS REGISTER  ⛔ MANDATORY SESSION READING (read in full, every session)
Consolidated register of SETTLED decisions — things NOT to relitigate. Built 2026-06-22 from LORAMER_ESSENCE.md,
LORAMER_HANDOFF.md (gospel/non-negotiables + lessons 1–59 + standing principles + bedrock), CLAUDE.md, the
data-completeness docs (accepted caps), STRIPE_BILLING_PLAN.md, LORAMER_WOO_CAPTURED_E1_V1.md,
docs/GOVERNMENT_DATA_REQUEST_POLICY.md, LORAMER_NAV_REGROUP_PLAN.md, and locked decisions in CONTINUE_HERE/design
docs. One line per decision: decision | source | "do not relitigate". Nothing invented. Conflicts resolved from
repo ground truth 2026-06-22. (The QUEUE of work lives in LORAMER_QUEUE_OF_RECORD.md.)
RULE: before proposing any action, restate the settled decisions here that bear on it — to prove you read them.

═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
GOVERNING LAW & BEDROCK (the rules that override everything)
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
- GOVERNING LAW: capture EVERYTHING from EVERYWHERE, store FOREVER (until cancel), at FULL grain WITH history — now, not "later." Only exception = a platform genuinely doesn't serve the data. "Future/phase/scope" deferral of any capture = a VIOLATION. | LORAMER_ESSENCE.md (top) | do not relitigate.
- BEDROCK — TOTAL DATA CAPTURE: on connecting ANY source, auto-capture+backfill every grain/dimension/metric to the deepest the platform allows + forward daily. ENFORCEMENT: (1) auto-backfill on connect, (2) forward covers ALL grains, (3) completeness gate gates "onboarded", (4) fetched-but-unpersisted = DEFECT. No session may narrow this. | LORAMER_HANDOFF.md:35-43 | do not relitigate.
- AUTO-BACKFILL-ON-CONNECT design: on connect → cursor 'pending' (never run inline); cron sweep drains lap-by-lap; 20-lap safety cap; honors per-day self-reconcile HALT; never oscillate into a wall (L51); managed=full speed, Woo=extra-gentle. | CONTINUE_HERE (queued 2026-06-18), ROADMAP Data-Completeness-Onboarding | do not relitigate (design settled; build pending).
- RIGHT > FAST, always. Senior-engineer (20+yr) rigor. NO same mistake twice. | HANDOFF Operator-Level-Truth | do not relitigate.
- EVERYTHING IS LAUNCH-CRITICAL by default for July 14, 2026 — only exceptions: named next-phase (design then write) or external constraint (Meta/Google review). | HANDOFF GOSPEL, ESSENCE | do not relitigate.
- DESKTOP=MOBILE PARITY — ONE responsive app, verify both per increment, never fork. | HANDOFF GOSPEL + STANDING | do not relitigate.
PARITY — CANONICAL DEFINITION (2026-07-08, Russ): the BARE word "parity" ALWAYS means MACHINE PARITY — the iMac and the MacBook Air can perform ALL the same tasks, fully. A machine that cannot complete a build/push/deploy gate the other can is a MACHINE-PARITY VIOLATION to fix, not a footnote to route around. All OTHER parities MUST carry an explicit qualifier and are never written as bare "parity": "desktop=mobile visual coherence" (one responsive app, both surfaces coherent, never forked — cf. :18); "-next↔live capability parity" (the cutover flip gate — a FLIP surface is at parity when it shows the same data, correctly, as the current app; cf. LORAMER_NEXT_FLIP_PROGRAM.md); "connection-state probe parity" (all 5 platforms probed equivalently); and misc technical axes (quota parity, forward/backfill parity, writer-behavior parity) which stay locally qualified at their use site. Rule: if a doc says "parity" with no qualifier, it means MACHINE parity; every non-machine sense REQUIRES its qualifier. | LORAMER_PARITY_GLOSSARY, 2026-07-08 | do not relitigate.
- The MOAT is the intelligence/memory/recommendations layer, NOT the dashboard. Goal = real-world recommendations & growth, not data display. | HANDOFF North-Star, ROADMAP Strategy | do not relitigate.
SEARCH-BEFORE-FORKING (2026-07-08, Russ): Before presenting ANY principle, default, product direction, or governing rule as if it needs deciding — i.e. before offering Russ a fork/option-menu on something that sounds like product law — Claude MUST first search past chats (conversation_search) and the repo docs for a prior decision. If it's already decided, CITE it and build to it; do NOT re-offer it as open. The tell that triggers this: Claude catching itself about to say "your call / here are the options" on anything resembling a settled principle. Re-litigating closed decisions is the single biggest waste of Russ's time. This session alone, mobile-first-standard, store-truth ROAS law, and "both ROAS labeled" were all already banked and got mistakenly re-opened. Searching first turns "let me propose this" into "you already decided this, building to it." | LORAMER_SEARCH_BEFORE_FORKING, 2026-07-08 | do not relitigate.
TWO-SOURCE VERIFY (2026-07-09, Russ): the relitigate/settled gate requires BOTH stores — the repo docs AND past chats (conversation_search). A single-store check is NOT "verified": searching the repo alone and finding nothing does NOT license "this is new/unsettled" (and vice-versa) — a decision can live in either store, so a one-store miss produces false "this is new" conclusions, the exact way settled calls get re-opened. Repo-only = still UNVERIFIED. Applies to SEARCH-BEFORE-NEW and SEARCH-BEFORE-FORKING alike: check both, or the gate did not pass. | LORAMER_TWO_SOURCE_VERIFY, 2026-07-09 | do not relitigate.
CONTRADICTION STOP (2026-07-09, Russ): when an instruction contradicts a banked GOVERNING LAW / ESSENCE / settled DECISION, Claude Code HALTS — it does NOT execute it, does NOT silently comply, and does NOT quietly "improve" it into compliance. It flags the contradiction in ONE line WITH the citation (which banked rule, and where), states the conflict plainly, and waits. The banked law wins over an in-the-moment instruction UNLESS Russ explicitly overrides it AS an override (knowingly changing the law) — not by accident. Executing a law-contradicting instruction is a failure even when the instruction came from Russ; the correct move is halt-flag-cite-wait, then let Russ confirm-as-override or restate. | LORAMER_CONTRADICTION_STOP, 2026-07-09 | do not relitigate.
PROOF-TARGET DEFAULT (2026-07-18, Russ): when a probe or Gate-A needs a client with REAL data, DEFAULT to FoamOh — clientId 957d484e-d0c4-4dd0-b382-d8499d556252 · Meta act_916558565662035 · Google Ads customer 7688521852 · GA4 properties/302128252 · Shopify dreamboard1.myshopify.com — window 2024, ESPECIALLY Nov–Dec 2024 (Black Friday peak, FoamOh's richest conversion history; recent windows read empty — it has ~$0 current Meta delivery). VERIFY FoamOh actually returns the SPECIFIC dimension being probed in that window BEFORE trusting it; fall back to a fleet sweep ONLY if genuinely absent, and SAY SO. Proof-target ONLY — changes NOTHING about capture (every client still gets everything, always). | LORAMER_PROOF_TARGET_FOAMOH_V1, 2026-07-18 | do not relitigate.
FIRST-CLASS DIMENSION DEFAULT (2026-07-18, Russ): anything Lora might PIVOT on gets its OWN breakdown_type (first-class, queryable via query_breakdown), NEVER folded into extra JSON — UNLESS a real reason (genuine unusable row-explosion, or it is not a queryable axis). Applies to EVERY fill going forward (the G-FILL/M-FILL/S-FILL/W-FILL/GA-FILL queue). extra JSONB is for NON-pivot attributes (ids, secondary labels, ratios), never for a dimension a user would slice by. (Supersedes the M-FILL#2 probe's field-widen-into-extra recommendation → attribution windows ship as a first-class breakdown_type.) | LORAMER_FIRST_CLASS_DIMENSION_DEFAULT_V1, 2026-07-18 | do not relitigate.
HISTORY-FIRST (2026-07-18, Russ): before ANY build / probe / spec on a topic, FIRST search the full chat history on that EXACT topic (conversation_search) + the repo docs, SYNTHESIZE what was already decided / built / attempted / rejected, and FOLD that synthesis INTO the Claude Code instruction itself — so no flight ever re-derives or re-litigates settled work. This makes the anti-relitigation discipline MANDATORY and UP-FRONT, not occasional-and-reactive: the synthesis is a required PRECONDITION of the instruction, the same forcing-function role the completion-gate plays for capture. Operationalizes DO-NOT-RELITIGATE and sits alongside SEARCH-BEFORE-FORKING (:21) + TWO-SOURCE VERIFY (:22) — those say "don't re-open a settled fork"; HISTORY-FIRST says "front-load the whole prior record before you even scope." | LORAMER_HISTORY_FIRST_V1, 2026-07-18 | do not relitigate.
DONE-MEANS-COMPLETE (2026-07-18, Russ): "done" for any capture family = COMPLETE against the AUDIT's vendor surface (docs/LORAMER_DATA_COMPLETENESS.md — every grain/dimension/metric the platform genuinely serves), NOT "a writer exists." This is ENFORCED IN CODE by the capture-completeness gate (scripts/check-capture-completeness.mjs, run in `npm run guard` = the pre-push gate): it FAILS the push when a `captured` family is a slice of the vendor surface or is captured-but-unwired from breakdown-registry.ts. THE GATE WINS: docs mirror the gate; if a doc says "done" and the gate disagrees, the gate is right. Work the fill queue IN ORDER; no new un-audited work jumps ahead of an open fill. Operationalizes BEDROCK/TOTAL-DATA-CAPTURE (:14) with a machine enforcer — a green writer is not done, a green gate is. | LORAMER_DONE_MEANS_COMPLETE_V1, 2026-07-18 | do not relitigate.
REPORT-TRANSFER DEFECT (2026-07-18, tracked infra issue — NOT repo-fixable): the Claude Code → chat report transfer TRUNCATES long / box-drawing / symbol-heavy payloads to BLANK. Regression began ~2026-07-18; the same reports transferred fine for ~3 months prior → it is an infra/harness-level regression, likely needs a Claude Code update / an Anthropic bug report, and CANNOT be fixed in this repo. INTERIM PROTOCOL (a tourniquet, not the fix): reports print SHORT — a one-line verdict + only the load-bearing evidence lines, in PLAIN ASCII (no box-drawing, no │═ , no emoji); the full detail is written to a file (e.g. /tmp/<task>.txt) that Russ opens directly. When the transfer is fixed, retire the tourniquet. | LORAMER_REPORT_TRANSFER_DEFECT_V1, 2026-07-18 | do not relitigate (until the infra fix lands).
LORA-COMPLETENESS AUDIT — NEXT MAJOR ARC (2026-07-18, Russ): after the capture fill queue, the next arc is the LORA-COMPLETENESS audit — the mirror of the capture-completeness audit, one layer up: does Lora actually USE everything captured? Six layers — (1) queryable, (2) retrieved, (3) in-context, (4) interpreted, (5) cross-referenced, (6) remembered. LAW: like the capture audit, it MUST terminate in a CODE GATE (a forcing function that fails when a captured dimension is not Lora-reachable/used), not a doc. Queued as the next arc after the fill queue (QUEUE owns the work item). | LORAMER_LORA_COMPLETENESS_AUDIT_V1, 2026-07-18 | do not relitigate.

LORA-GROUNDING GATE — THE ARC'S FIRST ENFORCER (2026-07-20): scripts/check-lora-grounding.mjs is wired into `npm run guard` (therefore into `npm run build`, the pre-push gate) and enforces two assertions the capture gate structurally cannot see — (a) DISCOVERABILITY: every tool-facing type in the registry-GENERATED query_breakdown enum must also be named in that tool's HAND-WRITTEN prose, because an enum member with no prose is reachable in principle and invisible in practice; (b) CAVEAT-PRESENCE: every LOAD-BEARING family must carry a non-empty `note`, where load-bearing is DERIVED from the registry's own flags (additive:false — summing it yields a confident wrong number · highCardinality:true — wide reads truncate and a truncated ranking reads as complete · rankBy:'conversions' — the SPEND_ZERO families whose spend is 0 by construction). DELIBERATE NARROWING, settled: bare geo membership does NOT require a per-entry note — the ad-location-vs-user-location caveat is owned in full by the geoScope/geoGrain tool descriptions, and asserting it on all 23 geo entries would manufacture 23 copies of one fact, the exact pathology the DOC-OWNERSHIP gates exist to stop; geo rows carrying real per-family risk are still caught by the high-cardinality rule (all 8 of the red findings were geo rows). Flip REQUIRE_NOTE_ON_ALL_GEO in the script to demand them. Baseline KNOWN_LORA_GAPS is EMPTY and two-way (a novel gap fails; a stale baseline entry also fails) so the queue can only shrink. THE LIMIT, on the gate's own green line: this proves a type is NAMED and a risky family ANNOTATED — it does NOT prove Lora reasons correctly, which is the eval set's question and beyond any static gate. | LORAMER_LORA_GROUNDING_GATE_V1, 2026-07-20 | do not relitigate.

═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
NON-NEGOTIABLE BRAND COMMITMENTS (commitments win over product decisions)
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
- DEEP KNOWLEDGE: LoraMer = "lore + sea" = accumulated deep knowledge; every feature judged on whether it makes Claude know the customer better. | HANDOFF/ROADMAP Brand | do not relitigate.
- A REAL HUMAN, ALWAYS: every customer reaches a real person on every plan, every time — operational not just marketing (Project 15 SLAs). | ROADMAP Project 15, HANDOFF | do not relitigate.
- PROMPT HONESTY: Lora never says what it can't show/prove; connected-but-empty surfaced explicitly; never fabricate (margin, audience, $0 false-zero). | ESSENCE, L11/L46/L47 | do not relitigate.
- CONNECTION-STATE CLAIMS REQUIRE A LIVE PROBE: no session writes "broken/dead/frozen/blocked/unhealthy" about a connection into any handoff doc from a stored flag alone; stored health flags are hypotheses until a live capture-path probe proves them. ENFORCED IN CODE across all 5 platforms (2026-06-24): meta/google + woocommerce are probe-gated in recordConnectionAuthFailure (a live probe before any 'reconnect' write — LORAMER_CONNECTION_PROBE_BEFORE_FLIP_V1 / _WOO_V1); shopify + ga are PROVEN-SAFE-BY-CONSTRUCTION (their credential flip comes only from a real refresh-determination or a live-API 401, narrow scope — WS2 #2b audit) — do NOT re-audit this ground. | Lesson 60, ESSENCE "never say what it can't prove" | do not relitigate.
- CLAIM-CONFIDENCE RULE (binds chat-Claude every session AND Lora in-product; obey, do NOT narrate): Before stating any factual claim, rule, or generalization, internally classify it — VERIFIED (read this session: docs/code/DB, or just searched — state plainly, cite source only when it adds something), DERIVED (reasoned from something verified — show the reasoning step, not a label; if the derivation can't be shown it's a guess, not derived), or UNVERIFIED (memory / pattern-match / clean-sounding generalization not checked — do NOT state as fact; either verify first via search/read/probe, or say "I haven't verified this — want me to check?" and stop). Banned move: stating an unverified claim in a confident, verified-sounding voice. A tidy authoritative-sounding generalization is the HIGHEST-risk case — treat the urge to state a clean rule as the trigger to verify, not to assert. Do NOT label sentences as ceremony; the classification is an internal gate on whether/how to speak. Surface uncertainty ONLY when a claim is actually unverified. Test every claim: could I show where this came from right now? If no → check it or flag it, never assert. | Root cause: 2026-06-24 session, repeated confident-but-unverified assertions (e.g. "breadth has no purge clock", "I can't see your settings") each corrected only by Russ pushing back — the exact failure this rule exists to stop. | do not relitigate.
- CLAIM-CONFIDENCE — HARD VERIFY GATE: Any factual claim, rule, or generalization that a build, commit, deploy, or recommendation depends on, and that is not VERIFIED this session (read/searched), MUST be verified through Claude Code BEFORE proposing action — not "want me to check?", check first. Never build, commit, or recommend from memory, pattern, or a prior-session summary. Unverified + load-bearing = stop and verify, every time. (Non-load-bearing asides may still be flagged as unverified.) | 2026-07-03 (strengthens the claim-confidence rule above; supersedes the "want me to check?" wording for load-bearing claims) | do not relitigate.
- DESTRUCTIVE-ACTION GATE (2026-06-29; governing law; sits with Claim-Confidence + Connection-Probe-Before-Flip; binds RECOMMENDATIONS, not just executions): before RECOMMENDING any remove / revoke / delete / disable / rotate-that-breaks / unlink — of access, tokens, accounts, OAuth grants, MCC/user links, data, rows, or ANYTHING touching a live platform review or pending submission (Google/Meta/Shopify/Stripe) — Claude MUST first verify WHY the thing exists / whether it's load-bearing (search repo docs + past sessions) and STATE the result. (1) absence-of-memory is NOT clearance, only VERIFIED-no-reason; (2) the check's result must be stated in the recommendation; (3) if it can't be run, WITHHOLD the recommendation (no caveat-and-give); (4) binds recommendations — telling Russ to do a destructive console action is gated; (5) feasible because it fires ONLY on the destructive class, not every turn. Full verbatim text: LORAMER_HANDOFF.md 4c-DESTRUCTIVE. | Root cause: 2026-06-29 recommended removing demo@'s MCC access as security cleanup; demo@ was load-bearing (pending Google Tool Change Form + permanent onboarding test fixture); a targeted search would have caught it, absence-of-memory did not — the exact failure this gate exists to stop. | do not relitigate.
- VERIFICATION LAW 1 — REAL-ENTRY-PATH PROOF (2026-07-11, load-bearing; same tier as CLAIM-CONFIDENCE): a deterministic Gate-A proof MUST start at the REAL entry point (login / HTTP route / page load) and traverse EVERY gate/guard/middleware/filter to the answer — NEVER call the target function directly and call that a pass. If the promise is "a member logs in and sees their client," simulate LOGIN → routing → resolution → result. Isolated-function proofs are NECESSARY, never SUFFICIENT. | Root cause: 2026-07-11, TWICE — resolveAccess passed in isolation, then live-FAILED (Lora: /api/intelligence fail-closed on a network blip; member login: welcome-gate + preview-gate + legacy owner-only list bounced the member BEFORE resolveAccess ran). "Proved the engine, never proved the car can drive." | do not relitigate.
- VERIFICATION LAW 2 — FULL-PATH RECON (2026-07-11): before building any flight touching a request / login / data flow, the read-only recon MUST enumerate EVERY gate/guard/middleware/filter between the user and the target; the fix must account for ALL of them. A recon that lists only the edited files is INCOMPLETE. | 2026-07-11 (from the same double miss) | do not relitigate.
- VERIFICATION LAW 3 — WHOLE-SURFACE SCOPING (2026-07-11): every flight states the COMPLETE user-facing behavior it promises, and Gate-A proves THAT end-to-end — not just the edited sub-piece. If a change implies adjacent behavior, NAME and PROVE it; never assume it done. | 2026-07-11 | do not relitigate.
- VERIFICATION — HONEST LIMIT (2026-07-11; bank, don't oversell): Laws 1–3 harden the class "isolated-logic-passes-but-live-path-fails"; they do NOT make verification infallible for NEW failure classes. On-device Gate-B on REAL data stays the MANDATORY final backstop (it caught BOTH 2026-07-11 misses within minutes). | 2026-07-11 | do not relitigate.

OPERATING PROTOCOL (how we work — settled)
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
- SESSION START GATE is the ONE authoritative resume protocol: ESSENCE → CONTINUE_HERE + REQUIRED READING (in full) → HANDOFF + ROADMAP → state NEXT STEP → wait for "go". Act only on what's read THIS session; repo is sole source of truth; panel+memory lag. | HANDOFF:1-31 | do not relitigate.

- END-OF-DAY TERSENESS (2026-07-10, Russ): after giving Russ the code/paste for the NEXT action, STOP — no "what's next" preview, no holding-open / next-steps lists, no postamble; he gets it in the next exchange anyway (redundant). Half the words in general; keep momentum. SHARPENS the standing terse-delivery rule (HANDOFF:189/276 — no recaps, no "here's what I'm going to do," just the step) with the post-paste angle — not a new independent rule. (Build-parity corollary already banked, do NOT duplicate: HANDOFF:374 — never name a machine in a build command; `npm run build` runs on EITHER.) | LORAMER_EOD_TERSENESS, 2026-07-10 | do not relitigate.

Sharpens EVERYTHING-LAUNCH-CRITICAL (:17) + RIGHT>FAST (:16) with the concrete mission. | LORAMER_LAUNCH_POSTURE_V1, 2026-07-10 | do not relitigate.
- TERSENESS — HARD (LORAMER_LAUNCH_POSTURE_V1, 2026-07-10, Russ): kill editorializing — NO timeline commentary, NO "big lift" asides, NO restating what was just said, NO postamble. Deliver RESULT + DECISION + PASTE; elaborate ONLY when asked; half the words. Sharpens the standing terse-delivery rules (HANDOFF:189/276) + END-OF-DAY TERSENESS (LORAMER_EOD_TERSENESS above). | LORAMER_LAUNCH_POSTURE_V1, 2026-07-10 | do not relitigate.
- ONE CHANGE IN FLIGHT — HARD (LORAMER_LAUNCH_POSTURE_V1, 2026-07-10, Russ): NEVER send Russ a paste while a Claude Code flight is running — WAIT for the report. One change in flight at a time. Sharpens ONE-THING-AT-A-TIME (HANDOFF operating-discipline) with the no-paste-mid-flight rule. | LORAMER_LAUNCH_POSTURE_V1, 2026-07-10 | do not relitigate.
- COMPLETENESS BEFORE BUILD (LORAMER_LAUNCH_POSTURE_V1, 2026-07-10, Russ): audit the FULL surface before scoping any build to it; a build plan is VALID only once its target surface is PROVABLY COMPLETE (the exhaustive-read gate — e.g. the 4197-line /dashboard read before the drill-spine scope). WITHHOLD a paste/action ONLY for (a) a genuine product question/decision the docs don't already answer, or (b) an outstanding in-flight report not yet returned. Absent those, proceed. An explicit "hold / Gate-A only / report first" instruction OVERRIDES this for that flight. | LORAMER_EOD_BANK_2026_07_11_V1, 2026-07-11 | do not relitigate.
- PLAIN-ENGLISH RUSS ACTIONS (2026-07-11, Russ): when Russ must DO something (a console action, a screenshot, a manual step), state it as plain-English bullets — NEVER bury a human action inside prose or a code block. Non-coder; the ask must be scannable. | LORAMER_EOD_BANK_2026_07_11_V1, 2026-07-11 | do not relitigate.
- MID-FLIGHT REQUESTS (2026-07-11, re-affirmed after a re-violation today): while a paste/flight report is OUTSTANDING, a NEW request from Russ — even an UNRELATED one — gets NO new paste; acknowledge it "logged, holding" and act only after the in-flight report returns. - ORG-IDENTITY MODEL (Path A, LOCKED 2026-07-11): an ORG = a LOGIN IDENTITY, not a platform-connection email. ONE login → ONE org → MANY clients. Platform tokens (Google MCC email, personal FB, Shopify partner/staff email) are CHILD data-sources per client, NEVER identity — multi-email platform connections are the NORMAL case, handled with ZERO special logic. A member's Lora/intelligence runs on the OWNER's data + tokens (share-runs-on-the-owner). Owner path stays byte-identical (ownerEmail===owner when the viewer IS the owner). | LORAMER_RBAC_ACCESS_ORG_V1 (37ccec3), 2026-07-11 | do not relitigate.
- FAIL-PARTIAL READ-PATH LAW (2026-07-11, from the "platform undefined" incident): read-path endpoints must fail PARTIAL + LOUD, NEVER fail-closed-silent. One source failing degrades THAT source (with a STRUCTURED log naming it) and still returns everything else; total context-blindness is a DEFECT. No silent .catch(()=>[]). A single flaky Supabase/API read must never blank a sibling source or the whole response. | LORAMER_INTELLIGENCE_HARDENING_V1 (064dd54), 2026-07-11 | do not relitigate.
- DELETE-CAPABILITY (proven 2026-07-11): scoped, children-first destructive delete is PROVEN on live data (Renee/Jason cleanup, ~1.52M rows, zero collateral, isolation manifest FIRST). This is the primitive for GDPR/CCPA user-delete + the live Meta data-deletion-callback compliance obligation; a real account+data delete is a manifest → children-first delete → verify-unchanged, NEVER a bare cascade-trust. | LORAMER_CLEANUP_DELETE_RENEE_JASON_V1, 2026-07-11 | do not relitigate.

- META ACTION-TYPE ALIAS CANONICALIZATION (SPEC): Meta reports one event under MULTIPLE parallel action_type aliases; any surface that sums or displays a Meta conversion value MUST project each alias family to ONE canonical alias per (base_event, entity_level, day) or it multi-counts. Required before ANY Meta value/ROAS card. Architecture = READ-LAYER projection only; RAW rows stay untouched forever (governing law). FULL SPEC in the sub-bullets below. | LORAMER_META_ALIAS_CANON, 2026-07-03 (full SPEC authored 2026-07-08 from DB-verified reads) | do not relitigate.
    · PROBLEM (DB-verified 2026-07-08): Meta emits one event under many parallel action_type strings. view_content = 7 aliases (view_content, omni_view_content, offsite_conversion.fb_pixel_view_content, onsite_web_view_content, onsite_web_app_view_content, onsite_app_view_content, onsite_conversion.view_content); 5 are value-bearing and ALL carry IDENTICAL value per client (The Escential Group c39ee088 $62,274; Shelley Kyle 23c697bb $69,067; account grain, last 30d). Naive SUM multi-counts 5× (Shelley $345,335 vs true $69,067).
    · KEY FINDING (verified 2026-07-08; REVISES the 2026-07-03 assumption): the dollar VALUE is PICK-INVARIANT — every value-bearing alias in a family carries the SAME value, so the canonical choice does NOT change reported REVENUE; it changes only the CONVERSION COUNT (and therefore the CPA/ROAS denominators). Lower-stakes than the 2026-07-03 note implied.
    · CANONICAL RULE: per (base_event, entity_level, day) pick ONE canonical alias — prefer omni_<event>, else offsite_conversion.fb_pixel_<event>, else bare <event>. omni_<event> = the deduped cross-surface SUPERSET (DB-verified: omni COUNT = offsite/bare count + onsite_app count — Shelley 1368+57=1425, Escential 1233+9=1242; value identical). Scope value DISPLAY to ONE entity_level (account = canonical grain); action_type is multi-level — NEVER sum across levels.
    · RESOLVER = DATA-DRIVEN, NOT a hardcoded alias list: classify each raw action_type by PARSING → {base_event, prefix_class, is_custom}. Prefix vocabulary present today (NON-EXHAUSTIVE — only 6 of 14 Meta clients had rows in the 30d account-grain window; full history + campaign/adset/ad grains will surface more): bare · omni_ · offsite_conversion.fb_pixel_ · onsite_web_ · onsite_web_app_ · onsite_app_ · onsite_conversion.<event> · web_in_store_ · web_app_in_store_. Any action_type the resolver CANNOT classify MUST surface LOUDLY (logged/visible), NEVER be silently dropped — silent false-zero is the house pathology (L15/L47).
    · CUSTOM CONVERSIONS stay DISTINCT: client-defined suffixes (offsite_<event>_add_meta_leads, offsite_<event>_add_20_s_calls) and onsite_conversion.<custom> are the client's OWN named conversions — keep as distinct labeled conversions, EXCLUDE from the standard-event canonical sum, NEVER drop, NEVER fold into the base event. ALL-means-ALL preserved.
    · NON-ALIASED ENGAGEMENT SINGLES stay distinct (not a family, never collapsed): page_engagement, post_engagement, link_click, video_view, post_reaction, like, comment, photo_view, post, post_interaction_*, onsite_conversion.post_*, onsite_conversion.messaging_*.
    · ARCHITECTURE: read-layer projection ONLY — a canonical-alias map applied at query/display time, analogous to the existing publisher_platform→placement BREAKDOWN_ALIAS (docs/LORAMER_BREAKDOWN_REGISTRY.md). RAW alias rows STAY untouched forever (governing law: capture EVERYTHING); NO capture-path change; NO resolver code in this spec (doc only).
    · OPEN / UNVERIFIED (non-blocking; flag before user-facing use): (a) EXTERNAL PEG not yet confirmed — omni_ count vs the figure Meta Ads Manager officially reports for the event is internal-reconciliation-only so far; non-blocking because value is pick-invariant; confirm opportunistically for count/CPA trust. (b) ViewContent VALUE SEMANTICS (what the value parameter represents / attribution window / user-dedup) is explained from Meta pixel docs but NOT independently reconfirmed — relevant to Lora's DISPLAY wording/tooltip later, not to the resolver; verify before any user-facing claim about what the number MEANS.
- CONVERSION-ACTION CAPTURE FLOOR (ACTIVE WORKSTREAM, ahead of P2-C): Every conversion action a client actually uses — phone calls, form fills, add-to-cart and every ecommerce action, purchases — stock AND custom, captured per action, WITH value where it exists, at campaign grain, on BOTH Google and Meta, forward and reliably regardless of volume. Small volume is never an excuse for a gap = an ALL-means-ALL violation. STILL PENDING = the shared query_breakdown chat-tool promotion (STOP-and-confirm shared-read-path). The dedicated MULTI-SOURCE ROAS CARD is now ✅ BUILT 2026-07-09 (LORAMER_NEXT_ROAS_CARD_V1 — see its dedicated line below). - MULTI-SOURCE ROAS CARD (2026-07-09): ✅ BUILT — LORAMER_NEXT_ROAS_CARD_V1, -next only, additive, NO migration. Data via a STANDALONE read (src/lib/next/roas-bases.ts + /api/next/card-roas → queryRoasBases): reconstructs from RAW per-day omni_purchase rows — the ONLY purchase_roas carrier; the sibling purchase aliases (purchase/onsite_web_purchase/offsite_conversion.fb_pixel_purchase/web_in_store_purchase) are identical-value DEDUP VIEWS, NEVER summed; a null/0-ROAS day is SKIPPED, not a zero. queryBreakdown + its dormant metaRoas carrier LEFT UNTOUCHED (shared/live read-path). --- docs/GOVERNMENT_DATA_REQUEST_POLICY.md (adopted 2026-06-11; owner Cote LLC d/b/a Cote Media; each principle its own line) ---
- (1) LEGALITY REVIEW: every public-authority request reviewed for valid legal process/jurisdiction/scope before any response; no data on an informal/voluntary/unverified request; invalid/improper requests refused. | GOV §1 | do not relitigate.
- (2) CHALLENGE: requests believed unlawful/overbroad/improper are challenged (narrow/quash via legal channels; counsel engaged as needed). | GOV §2 | do not relitigate.
- (3) DATA MINIMIZATION: where disclosure is legally compelled, disclose only the minimum data the process requires. | GOV §3 | do not relitigate.
- (4) DOCUMENTATION: every request documented + retained (request, legal reasoning, parties, response). | GOV §4 | do not relitigate.
- (5) USER NOTIFICATION: where lawful, affected users are notified of requests concerning their data. | GOV §5 | do not relitigate.

- SHOPIFY TOKEN FACTS (verified 2026-07-01): (a) The app-path OAuth token exchange (getValidShopifyToken) is reinstall-SAFE for a store with a CLEAN SINGLE shopify_tokens row — it is NOT universally safe. (b) The forced-reinstall risk is DUPLICATE shopify_tokens rows (multiple user_email keys) racing Shopify's one-time-use refresh token — NOT the act of minting. (c) Real customer stores were clean this session (dreamboard1/influential minted without reinstall). (d) The Shopify Partner dashboard is the source of truth for CLIENT_ID + CLIENT_SECRET — NOT the iMac. (e) A mint requires BOTH SHOPIFY_CLIENT_ID and SHOPIFY_CLIENT_SECRET real. - [LESSON 2026-07-15 — why the original audit undercounted 3→6] A PARTIAL grain filter is an INDEX MISS THAT LOOKS CORRECT. The 8s audit grepped for latest-date queries with NO grain filter and found 3; it missed ga-overview (entity_level only), money and store-detect (missing breakdown_value) because they LOOK filtered. A partial index is all-or-nothing: the planner must prove the query implies the FULL predicate (all three columns) or the index is silently unusable. When auditing partial-index coverage, grep for the sites that DON'T match the predicate EXACTLY — not for the ones with no filter at all. | LORAMER_LATEST_DATE_ACCOUNT_GRAIN_V1, 2026-07-15 | do not relitigate.

- [CORRECTION 2026-07-15 — of my own entry, caught by RUSS not by me] The wrap commit ce7500d banked "[DECIDED, RUSS] SOFT LAUNCH MOVED OFF JULY 22, new target TBD." **Russ never said that.** He moved the target TO July 22 earlier the same day, and separately said he would ACCEPT a delay if the GA submission caused one. I converted a tolerated outcome into a stated decision, invented a "new target TBD," and marked the real July 22 entry SUPERSEDED — all of it fabricated. Both are corrected in place above. This violated the CLAIM-CONFIDENCE law that was added to ESSENCE **in the very same commit**, which is the sharpest available proof that writing a law is not the same as obeying it. | LORAMER_SOFT_LAUNCH_JULY22_V1, 2026-07-15 | do not relitigate.
- [SUBMITTED 2026-07-15] analytics.readonly DECLARED on the shared consent screen + justification + a new demo video covering BOTH OAuth clients. ACCEPTED RISK: re-verification of the already-approved adwords app. EXPECT revocation across ALL 11 GA connections during the transition (all 11 ride ONE grant on cotebrandmarketing@gmail.com — one account × one client = one grant). Remedy is re-auth via /api/ga/start: non-destructive, and prompt=consent forces the screen every time so a re-auth always works. Not a defect if it happens — it is the priced-in, knowingly-accepted cost of submitting NOW rather than after the cohort lands (see the STANDS entry above; the July 22 target is UNCHANGED). | LORAMER_SOFT_LAUNCH_JULY22_V1, 2026-07-15 | do not relitigate.
- [VERIFIED 2026-07-15, three independent ways — both client IDs' leading segment read from the LIVE production redirects, plus the Console Clients tab] OAUTH TOPOLOGY: exactly TWO OAuth clients, ONE project (928420234811 / savvy-palace-495920-v2). "Cote Media Ads Manager" = GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID (sign-in AND the Ads decoupler SHARE it — auth.ts:19 + google-ads/connect/start:31). "LoraMer GA Connector" = GOOGLE_ANALYTICS_CLIENT_ID (ga/start:24, ga/callback:73, ga-token.ts:62). Verification and the declared-scope list are PROJECT-level: two clients, ONE consent screen, ONE verification state. THEREFORE a separate CLIENT buys NO isolation — one already exists and bought none. This RETIRES the CONTINUE_HERE:784 escalation ("possibly a dedicated GA OAuth client off the verified-Ads project"): its value was always the "off the project" half, not the "dedicated client" half. Only a separate PROJECT would isolate, and that is ruled out. | LORAMER_OAUTH_TOPOLOGY_V1, 2026-07-15 | do not relitigate.
- [CORRECTION 2026-07-15] The 2026-05-30 GA connector design (docs/GA_CONNECTOR_DESIGN_2026_05_29.md) filed GA verification as "not a V1 blocker, revisit past ~100 users." That decision was never PROMOTED onto the pre-launch checklist when the June adwords submission went out, so analytics.readonly was never added to it — which is why the scope sat undeclared for weeks and surfaced as an "intermittent unverified-app warning" nobody could place. Superseded by the submission above. ROOT CAUSE, generalize it: a filed "future concern" with NO TRIGGER to re-check is a silent liability. A deferral needs a trip-wire (a date, a threshold, or a checklist line), or it is just forgetting with extra steps. | LORAMER_OAUTH_TOPOLOGY_V1, 2026-07-15 | do not relitigate.
- [CORRECTION 2026-07-15, measured] The 8s live-ceiling audit banked "heavy read null, light read a date." That is TRUE only for SINGLE-CLIENT routes (client-metrics: Champion returned 2026-07-14 pre-fix). It is FALSE for /api/clients/metrics: its Promise.all FAN-OUT across all 20 accessible clients meant the heavy clients' 8s scans took the light ones down with them, so lastActive was null for ALL clients, Champion included — the live-path route was blank for 100% of the cohort, not just the heavy tail. Proven by a measured BEFORE at 45781ff (throwaway worktree, same machine, same DB): 14,107ms, every lastActive null. Only visible because BEFORE was MEASURED rather than recalled from the note. | LORAMER_LATEST_DATE_ACCOUNT_GRAIN_V1, 2026-07-15 | do not relitigate.
- [LESSON 45 BROADENED 2026-07-15] `vercel env pull` now blanks ALL 46 user-defined vars, not just the handful listed at HANDOFF:391 — only Vercel's own turbo/NX vars carry values. The Vercel MCP has NO env-read tool (it exposes projects/deployments/logs/toolbar only). CONSEQUENCE, and the useful half: to confirm what production actually SENDS, read the LIVE PRODUCTION REDIRECT (e.g. GET /api/ga/start → 307 → parse client_id/scope from the Location header) — that is STRONGER evidence than the settings panel anyway, because it proves what the running code emits rather than what is filed. Corollary: prod's NEXTAUTH_SECRET is unreadable, so a prod-cookie Gate-B is IMPOSSIBLE from Claude Code; the house pattern (live dev server + minted cookie against the SAME prod DB) is the real-path proof, and it is faithful for DB-ceiling questions because the 8s statement_timeout is enforced by PostgREST, not Vercel. | LORAMER_LATEST_DATE_ACCOUNT_GRAIN_V1, 2026-07-15 | do not relitigate.

═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
LESSONS 1–67 (+ 1 dated 2026-06-22) — failure modes that bit this project; never repeat. (LORAMER_HANDOFF.md)
COUNT: 65 numbered lessons, sequence 1–65 CONTIGUOUS, NO GAPS (lesson 26 is split 26a/26b — HANDOFF dual-numbered it) + 1 dated lesson (2026-06-22) + 2 early pre-numbered patterns below.
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
1 Silent-skip via shared marker — use content-based idempotency, not marker-presence.
2 Scope assumption in destructure adds — grep the prop in the parent's scope before patching.
3 TS prop add = two edits/component (destructure AND type; check inline vs multi-line type).
4 JS string apostrophes break builds — use backticks for any string with apostrophes/quotes.
5 Stale anchors — re-read the current file (fresh sed/grep) before any patch.
6 Marker-collision silent failure — distinct sub-markers OR per-edit content checks.
7 Per-edit content-based idempotency check is the best practice (all/some/none present logic).
8 Silent .catch(()=>[]) hides GAQL/API errors — instrument console.error before concluding "no data".
9 Cache invalidation hides deployed fixes (15-min) — force a miss via a never-used date range.
10 Whitespace in anchors — bytes matter; dump exact bytes when an anchor fails.
11 PROMPT-AS-MIRROR — grounding/constraint text goes in code comments (INTERNAL_GROUNDING), never user-rendered prompt.
12 Meta breakdowns go in &breakdowns=, NOT &fields= (else HTTP 400, swallowed by .catch).
13 Same-line comment after a token (comma/paren/bracket/template) can break webpack — comment on its own line.
14 tsc --noEmit is NOT npm run build — webpack is stricter (syntax/mangled-literals); build is the real gate.
15 Surfacing raw API status/body into the prompt = diagnostic of last resort (always with a planned cleanup).
16 Anchor only from the current-turn paste — never memory/earlier turns/grep; small anchors over big.
17 Same-named files + blind mv is dangerous — unique-name downloads before moving.
18 Patch scripts with hardcoded machine paths silently fail — match the machine's path.
19 ONE canonical date resolver (resolveDateWindow) — never roll per-platform date math.
20 Revenue = NET (currentSubtotalPriceSet), not gross — surface refunds; match merchant analytics.
21 GAQL has NO LAST_90_DAYS enum — explicit BETWEEN via resolveDateWindow.
22 Platform selector and tab nav are separate controls — switch to Overview on platform change from a non-ad tab.
23 Safe Supabase dup cleanup — backup→repoint children→verify counts→delete only emptied twins (keeper isn't always older).
24 Bug hunts in big files — investigate-only first, then a tight fix spec.
25 Repo is single source of truth — git pull at session start on either machine; unpushed work doesn't travel.
26a Serverless backfill cross-request cursor race — do the whole job in ONE invocation (in-memory loop); persist the cursor only for resume-after-interrupt, never as the loop's control.
26b RightPanel renders TWIN desktop/mobile message containers (hidden md:flex / flex md:hidden, both in DOM) — audit BOTH when touching either; assign anchors by document order. (HANDOFF dual-numbered both as "26"; HANDOFF:730 explicitly notes the collision.)
27 zsh eats unquoted globs (quote --include="*.ts"); BSD grep needs -E for alternation.
28 Verify a freshly-deployed cron/route against LIVE prod + give it time (stale triggers/logs mislead).
29 Heredoc/terminal pastes drop characters — deliver code as files; tsc catches mangled identifiers, NOT mangled string literals — grep critical strings.
30 Backfill depth = "as deep as the platform serves," discovered by probing; report the ACTUAL earliest row, never the swept cursor.
31 Per-platform retention differs — probe before trusting any documented limit.
32 JSX comments are {/* */}, not /* */ (renders as visible text; tsc misses it).
33 CRON-GATED VERIFICATION — deploy before the cron window or manually trigger after; capture writes YESTERDAY's date.
34 Handoffs anchor on session TAG + files, never a commit hash (local commits squash/rewrite on push).
35 pg_dump in CI: invoke by ABSOLUTE path /usr/lib/postgresql/17/bin/pg_dump (PATH resolves to v16).
36 GitHub Actions "Re-run jobs" replays the ORIGINAL commit — use a fresh workflow_dispatch to test a fix.
37 Vercel env rotation needs a redeploy to bind; native cron rotates atomically with the redeploy.
38 Supabase DB password is reset-safe (app uses API keys, not raw Postgres password).
39 A DB write that must touch a row → UPSERT (not UPDATE) + check affected count, log loudly on 0; never assume success on no-throw.
40 Never render internal flag/enum/status/tier keys to users — pass through a human-label map next to the keys.
41 Stripe Portal "cancel at period end" sets cancel_at (timestamp), not cancel_at_period_end (boolean).
42 Google OAuth consent-screen edits (name/domains/scopes/URLs/logo) trigger RE-verification — treat as frozen at launch.
43 Google Ads permissible-use update → Tool Change Form (zero-risk MCC metadata), not a full Standard Access app.
44 Walk every onboarding identity SHAPE with a real identity before launch (founder-only testing only exercises one shape).
45 vercel env pull can report a populated prod var as blank — verify "empty env" against runtime behavior; never re-set to a guess.
46 An API probe verifies what the CREDENTIAL can see, not what EXISTS — a boundary coinciding with a known limit IS the explanation; scope-truncated $0 rows are false-zeros, delete them.
47 EVERYTHING GETS EVERYTHING — completeness is a CORRECTNESS requirement; every gap explicit; any cap is a logged deliberate decision (MATRIX).
48 Vercel MCP has NO env-write — rotate via CLI (value via stdin, never printed) + redeploy to bind.
49 Guard NOT-NULL numerics vs NaN at the WRITE BOUNDARY (Number.isFinite), not per-builder (NaN ?? 0 = NaN → 23502 drops the whole row).
50 Don't conclude a logic bug from a symptom seen under one timing — replay the exact query against live DB; schedule catchup AFTER forward.
51 A backfill DRIVER LOOP must circuit-break on a persistent per-window error; never oscillate/re-walk; be gentle on live stores; resume from the TRUE frontier; when in doubt, STOP and surface.
52 Next.js App Router caches global fetch → deterministic supabase-js reads/writes go STALE/DROPPED on Vercel; force-no-store on read-after-write routes; a unique request body masks the bug.
53 A persisted UI selection carried across a context switch must be RE-VALIDATED against the new context (derive caps from the incoming entity); land on a valid non-blank default.
54 Shopify managed-install scopes come from the DEPLOYED config (shopify app deploy), not the authorize-URL scope= param; a refresh preserves the original grant; verify a scope change by PROBE.
55 A flag-gated reconnect control can be invisible exactly when needed — any re-auth/extend-scope affordance must be reachable for a HEALTHY connection.
56 shopify app deploy needs the Shopify CLI on node ≥22.12 (CLI 4.x crashes on node 20).
57 Vercel env VALUES are unreadable from tooling (masked / pull-blanked / no MCP env tool) — confirm from the source system.
58 Shopify revenue basis differs BY GRAIN — reconcile to the cent (per-line refund attribution + pro-rata residual) or ship a silent per-SKU lie; a WRONG basis is worse than no basis.
59 A permanent-history backfill must self-reconcile per unit of work (Σ within 1¢ each day BEFORE write/cursor-advance) or HALT loudly — never persist a bad day.
60 A connection-state claim (broken/dead/frozen/blocked/unhealthy/reconnect-needed) is a HYPOTHESIS until a LIVE capture-path probe proves it — never written as fact from a stored flag (health/last_error_code/oauth_190/last_ok_at) alone; stored flags are DERIVED CONTEXT, not ground truth (same class as the Google end_date adapter-semantics bug). Origin: a prior session wrote "Influential Drones Meta BLOCKED/broken/oauth_190" into CONTINUE_HERE + QUEUE + a session log as settled fact from the flag alone — it was FALSE (token alive HTTP 200, warehouse reconciled to the penny) and survived days because each session read it forward without re-deriving. RULE: before writing any negative connection claim into ANY handoff doc, run a live probe (raw HTTP status + body, Lesson 15 style); until probed, record "flag says X — UNVERIFIED, probe before acting," never as a blocker. The transient-190-dark-flags-a-live-connection generator = WS2 #2 (separate fix).
61 A backfill writer must STOP AT its retention floor as an EMPTY SUCCESS (per-adapter granularMonths clamp — meta=36, GA fixed 2015-08-14, Google account/dim 132mo), NOT overshoot into a boundary THROW; a step is 'done' ONLY on empty-success-at-floor or reconciled-to-floor, NEVER on an error. Origin: the onboarding drain's Meta account step stoppedOnError at the ~37mo boundary (it overshot to 132mo), never completed, and BLOCKED the later placement step. Fix (LORAMER_ONBOARD_DRAIN_V1): granularMonths=36 for meta; PER-STEP-INDEPENDENT drain (a stuck early step never starves a later one); verified Meta taxonomy decides done-vs-retry-vs-surface ({1,2,4,17,613,80004}/429/timeout=retry · 100·1487534=narrow-the-window · 100-other/190/368=surface · empty-success=done); NEVER swallow the fetch error (L15) — surface code/subcode so floor-vs-transient is distinguishable.
62 A platform-wide "stall" + a cluster of serverless TIMEOUTS with NO DB/lock/deadlock evidence and NO code-deploy correlation = look UPSTREAM at the external API's QUOTA, not infra. The 2026-06-28 Google 504 cluster (06:20–11:40Z) AND the 4h geo "stall" were ONE event: Basic-Access developer-scope quota exhaustion (15k ops/day, one shared dev token) burned by the morning geo fan-out by ~11:32Z. FALSIFIED, explicitly: (a) Supabase swap/memory and (b) BUDGET_MS 680 — neither caused nor fixed the 504s (they ran 5.5h AFTER the BUDGET_MS deploy and stopped exactly when geo WRITES stopped, 3.5h BEFORE the Supabase resize). Read the REAL error bytes (e.errors[0]) before blaming infra; correlate the symptom's start/stop against the actual workload, not against whatever was deployed.
63 A backfill lap that throws OR fails to advance but lets the cron fire return HTTP 200 is a SILENT failure — geo was DEAD 4h under green 200s with the error masked as "[object Object]". Every drain fire must return ok:false/failed:N + console.error for ANY thrown/non-200 lap (ALL platforms); a dead/non-advancing lap can never read as a green fire. (LORAMER_GOOGLE_QUOTA_GUARD_V1 CHANGE 1.)
64 TWO completion authorities that can disagree are a stall trap: the sync_state cursor backfill_complete (writer) vs platform_connections.onboard_steps_done (drain). A stale done-flag over an INCOMPLETE cursor silently SKIPS a client forever (Woo Shelley: onboard ['woo'] done but cursor complete=false at 2018-12-13, owes to ~2015 — frozen ~12d; drain pending filter excludes her). The cursor must be the SINGLE completion authority; any path that re-opens a cursor to complete=false MUST clear that step key from onboard_steps_done.
65 Woo backfill "complete = one 21-day all-status-ZERO chunk" (woocommerce-backfill.ts) can FALSE-complete on any >21-day sales GAP mid-history — a quiet store stretch reads as history-start. When hardening woo completeness: probe the store's true oldest order, or require N consecutive zero chunks.
66 supabase-js .upsert(row) WITHOUT an explicit { onConflict } defaults its conflict target to the PRIMARY KEY. On a table whose real dedup key is a SECONDARY UNIQUE column (meta_tokens: PK=id + UNIQUE(user_email)), and where the PK is auto-generated (no id in the row), the upsert becomes an INSERT → it violates the secondary UNIQUE → 409, and if the returned error is discarded the write is a SILENT no-op that still looks successful. This bit the Meta callback: re-auth never rewrote the token (only the first-ever connect worked; the success redirect fired regardless), which broke the cote@ refresh. RULE: every upsert names its conflict target explicitly ({ onConflict: '<the-unique-column>' }) AND checks the returned error (fail loud — redirect/throw, never swallow, per L15). Gate-A a token/dedup upsert by reproducing the SECOND write to an existing key: buggy path 409s, fixed path UPDATES in place (id stable, 1 row). (LORAMER_META_CALLBACK_ONCONFLICT_V1, 2026-07-02.)
67 A Gate-A that checks only STATUS CODES can pass while the BEHAVIOR is broken — assert the behavior the gate exists to protect, not just 2xx/3xx. Origin: the 138a820 legacy-surface middleware silently broke the signup funnel two ways and that flight's Gate-A missed BOTH because it only read status codes: (a) it gated /agency + /business — the PRE-AUTH two-door signup pages — so unauthenticated new users were bounced to /login (the pages' content was never read → public marketing doors misclassified as "legacy tenant pages"); (b) it redirected /dashboard → -next BEFORE the /dashboard layout's enforceWelcomeGate could fire, so a fresh user reached the empty -next portfolio with NO account_type (Add-client 409s). The Gate-A even OBSERVED break (b) — /dashboard-next/clients returned 200 for a brand-new no-account_type user — and read it as "not over-blocked" instead of "onboarding didn't fire." RULE: for any gate/redirect/auth change, (1) READ what each matched path actually IS before gating it (public vs authed, data vs marketing), and (2) assert the POSITIVE behavior it must preserve — "a fresh user is REDIRECTED to /welcome", "the door RENDERS unauthenticated" — a bare 200 or 307 is necessary, NEVER sufficient. (LORAMER_SIGNUP_FUNNEL_FIX_V1, 2026-07-14.)
(2026-06-22) Product/line grain must reconcile to its account grain on the SAME revenue basis; re-capture history ONLY AFTER the basis fix ships (re-capturing before forces a second pass — violates "never twice").

═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
EARLY "NO SAME MISTAKE TWICE" PATTERNS (HANDOFF Operator-Level-Truth, pre-numbered, overlap lessons above)
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
- Claude.ai cannot read the local repo — use Claude Code for whole-repo audits/multi-file reads; don't guess from the lagging project panel.
- Right > fast cost: doing it twice always costs more time than doing it right once + the emotional cost on Russ.

- [DECIDED 2026-07-16] **iOS FLEX MIN-WIDTH: A SCROLLER IS NOT ENOUGH.** `.md table` already carried overflow-x:auto — the scroll container was correct and in the right place the whole time. It was overruled because .bubbleAssistant, .scroll and .panel computed min-width:auto as flex items, so the table's intrinsic nowrap width propagated up the chain to .panel. .inputBar is a SIBLING of .scroll inside .panel, so a widened panel carries the send arrow off-screen with it — the arrow was never mis-placed, the panel was panned. ANY scroll container inside a flex chain needs min-width:0 on EVERY flex ancestor or it does not fire on iOS. Headless WebKit does NOT reproduce this — it honors the scroller. Fixed 2026-07-16 (LORAMER_NEXT_CHAT_FLEX_MINWIDTH_V1). | do not relitigate.
- [DECIDED 2026-07-16] **HEADLESS CANNOT SEE THE iOS FLEX BUG.** Playwright WebKit contained a 976px 10-col table at 390px BEFORE the fix — table scrolled internally, panel stayed 384, arrow at right 370, document 390 — while the bug was live on a real iPhone in BOTH Chrome and Safari at that exact moment. A headless render measurement is NOT evidence about this class of defect. Assert the CSS INVARIANT (min-width:0 present on the flex chain; overflow-x:auto on .md table and .md pre), or test on a real device. Do not build a guard whose green means nothing. | 2026-07-16 | do not relitigate.
- [DECIDED 2026-07-16] **REACT STATE LAGS INSIDE A SYNCHRONOUS CALLBACK.** react-grid-layout 1.5.3 fires onBreakpointChange THEN onLayoutChange in the SAME synchronous call stack (lib source ResponsiveReactGridLayout.onWidthChange, lines 174→175). A guard reading React STATE set by the first callback is STALE inside the second — setState batches/defers, refs do not. CardGrid's `isMobile` guard read the lagging `bp` state, so entering the sm breakpoint wrote the sm w:1 `stacked` layout into the DESKTOP layout, and autosave persisted it → the dashboard-cards-collapse-to-70px bug. FIX (LORAMER_NEXT_GRID_BP_REF_V1): read a bpRef.current set synchronously in onBreakpointChange. RULE: any guard inside a synchronous callback chain must read a REF, not state. Introduced 2026-07-09 (bfe1a3d), live one week, found 2026-07-16 by Russ noticing collapsed cards. | do not relitigate.
- [DECIDED 2026-07-16] **A CODE FIX DOES NOT HEAL WRITTEN DATA.** The sm-leak persisted w:1 desktop layouts into dashboard_layouts; fixing the leak stops NEW corruption but leaves the already-written rows (9, all one user, 2026-07-16) collapsed on every load. DETECT-AT-LOAD heals them: discard a desktop layout matching the corrupt signature (all items w:1 AND all x:0, ≥2 items) and fall back to the PAGE's own default (storeDefaultView on store pages, else defaultOverviewView) — no DB write, no migration, reversible, heals on next visit; the next autosave persists it. Chosen over a migration because only one user was affected. RULE: when shipping a fix for a bug that WRITES, always ask what is already written and heal it too. | LORAMER_NEXT_GRID_BP_REF_V1, 2026-07-16 | do not relitigate.
- [DECIDED 2026-07-16] **dvh DOES NOT SHRINK FOR THE KEYBOARD.** dvh/svh/lvh account for the browser's retractable ADDRESS BAR, NOT the on-screen keyboard. The keyboard shrinks the VISUAL viewport and leaves the LAYOUT viewport untouched, so a position:fixed sheet at 100dvh stays full-height and mis-registered against the visible area (the Ask-Lora sheet panned on keyboard dismiss). Nothing in the codebase listened to window.visualViewport — zero grep hits. BIND a fixed sheet to visualViewport (height + offsetTop) or it never knows where the screen is. The page bleeding through the gap (ClientPage's 'Change account', the ROAS rules box) was the SAME root: an opaque panel covering where it THINKS the screen is. FIX: LORAMER_NEXT_CHAT_VISUAL_VIEWPORT_V1 (visualViewport → CSS vars on .scrim, 100dvh fallback). | 2026-07-16 | do not relitigate.

- [DECIDED 2026-07-16] **A TRUE PREMISE IS NOT A DIAGNOSIS.** The visual-viewport chat fix (78de5ab) rested on two VERIFIED facts — dvh does not shrink for the keyboard, and nothing listened to visualViewport (zero grep hits). Both true. The fix DID NOT WORK (Russ confirmed on device, Chrome + Safari, unchanged). Facts that are true AND load-bearing-sounding are the most dangerous input to a build, because they survive every check except the one that matters: does the bug go away. Ship a fix ONLY when the failure has been REPRODUCED and the fix is proven against that reproduction. ★UI-OVERFLOW was never reproduced outside Russ's phone, and four theories in a row were wrong — each internally coherent, each derived from a CSS read + an engine that does not reproduce the bug. | 2026-07-16 | do not relitigate.
- [LESSON 2026-07-16] **POSTGREST 1000-ROW CAP.** Any PostgREST RPC that returns SETOF (a table / rows) is SILENTLY capped at 1000 rows AND returned UNORDERED — no error, no warning. An aggregation that can exceed 1000 groups MUST return a SINGLE jsonb array (the groups live inside one scalar row, where the row cap cannot bite). Caught in G2 2A Gate-A by a distinct-count mismatch (1000 vs 3169): a top-N over a silently-truncated, unordered set is a WRONG ANSWER THAT LOOKS RIGHT. The SETOF→jsonb return-type change needs DROP+CREATE, not CREATE OR REPLACE. | LORAMER_BREAKDOWN_SQL_AGG_V1, 2026-07-16 | do not relitigate.
- [DECISION 2026-07-16] **SQL-SIDE BREAKDOWN AGGREGATION = the scoped RPC `query_breakdown_agg`** (migration 038): a client-anchored GROUP BY on idx_metrics_daily_client_platform_bt_level_date, returning a jsonb array of (breakdown_value, parent_entity_id, sums); the JS layer keeps entity-level scope + canonicalize + sort + value-ASC tiebreak + topN + notes, so it stays byte-identical to the cent. PostgREST `db-aggregates-enabled` was REJECTED — it opens aggregate surface on EVERY table for EVERY caller to solve one query. Do NOT raise again. | LORAMER_BREAKDOWN_SQL_AGG_V1, 2026-07-16 | do not relitigate.
- [LESSON 2026-07-16] **PAYLOAD IS A GATE, NOT JUST TIME.** G2 2B measured DB execution at ~300ms (passing an 8s check) while the return-all-groups RPC handed back **32.4 MB** for ga_landing_page — 80,638 groups moved over the wire and JSON-parsed in a serverless lambda to return 20 rows. An 8s statement_timeout check ALONE would have waved this through. MEASURE THE PAYLOAD, not just execution time. Fix: a bounded topN RPC (migration 039) does ORDER BY + LIMIT in SQL → 4.5 KB. | LORAMER_BREAKDOWN_BOUNDED_TOPN_V1, 2026-07-16 | do not relitigate.
- [DECISION 2026-07-16] **BOUNDED-RPC SCOPE.** bounded = a NEW tool type (geo / ga_* / age_gender) OR an existing type at an EXPLICITLY-requested entityLevel (Google device/hour at ad_group/keyword). The 14 EXISTING tool types at their DEFAULT level keep the all-groups path, preserving byte-identical (proven, 6 pairs). The bounded branch THROWS if canonicalize is set — canonicalization collapses values AFTER aggregation, so a SQL topN taken BEFORE the collapse ranks the wrong things. Never silently degrade. | LORAMER_BREAKDOWN_BOUNDED_TOPN_V1, 2026-07-16 | do not relitigate.
- [DECISION 2026-07-16] **A GENERATED SCHEMA CHANGES WHAT A GUARD CAN PROVE.** Once claude-tools' query_breakdown enums GENERATE from breakdown-registry, "schema == registry" is tautological — a guard comparing them is always green. The drift guard was re-pointed at REGISTRY INTEGRITY (both files generate from the registry; every geo tuple is resolvable) — a real, build-blocking failure, proven RED with `next build` never running. Live-DB reachability (is every CAPTURED tuple declared?) stays the SEPARATE non-build script (scripts/breakdown-reachability-check.mjs) — CI has no network. - [LAW, 2026-07-17] **THE FRESHNESS GATE PROVES THE DIGEST MATCHES ITS SOURCES. IT DOES NOT PROVE THE SOURCES AGREE WITH EACH OTHER.** On 2026-07-17 the session opened with a stale RUSS ACTION #1 telling Russ to go read a Google OAuth rejection surface for a gate closed the day before. CONTINUE_HERE:580 said OPEN. DECISIONS:443 said CLOSED. Every hash was green, 10/10, the whole time. ROOT CAUSE: the 07-16 OAuth-closure paste wrote the ledger and regenerated the digest but never updated CONTINUE_HERE, which is what §E reads from. The gate could not catch it because it is a consistency check, not a truth check. RULE: when a decision CLOSES something, update EVERY doc that asserts it is open — the ledger AND the resume path. Grep the closed thing by name across all gated source docs before the wrap. The ledger is authoritative when docs conflict, but a conflict that reaches a session opener has already cost the session. THIS IS THE THIRD TIME IN TWO DAYS: SECURITY_POSTURE 7.9 claimed the reviewer login was dead infra (wrong, and would have talked a session into deleting live Shopify compliance); claude-tools.ts:118 described entityLevel behavior the code did not have; chat.module.css:3 claimed 100dvh shrinks for the keyboard. FOURTH INSTANCE, same day: the digest's §H open-queue index sliced the QUEUE at a "DONE — DO NOT REBUILD" marker and silently omitted EVERY item banked after 2026-07-15 — the entire G2 arc's follow-ons, ★UI-OVERFLOW, ★SUPPORT-BOT, and the rest. The items were in the QUEUE file the whole time and every hash was green. A condensed index is a CLAIM about completeness. If it cannot defend that claim, it must not be presented as the open list. Fixed 2026-07-17 (LORAMER_DIGEST_H_COMPLETENESS_V1: §H now selects OPEN items by meaning — block-grouped, terminal-status, position-independent — with only the fenced DONE appendix skipped; proven by a deliberate below-marker append that the old slice dropped and the new extractor catches). ⚠ STILL UNFIXED, SAME CLASS, NAMED NOT FIXED: §C's honesty-clause selector in build-resume-digest.mjs takes essence.split('\\n').filter(l=>l.trim()).slice(-1)[0] — the LAST non-empty line of ESSENCE by POSITION; append anything after the honesty clause and the digest silently swaps in the wrong line. - [LAW, 2026-07-17] **NEVER WRITE DOWN A FACT ANOTHER DOC OWNS. POINT AT THE OWNER.** 2026-07-17 recon: ~10 volatile facts were hand-copied into ~27 live places across ESSENCE, HANDOFF, SECURITY_POSTURE and CONTINUE_HERE §E. Five of the seven silent-drift defects of 07-16/17 lived in ONE class — external approval status (Meta/Shopify/OAuth/Standard-Access). The doc set is NOT overbuilt; each doc has a unique authoritative core (ESSENCE=law · DECISIONS=status/ledger · QUEUE=backlog · CONTINUE_HERE=narrative+opener · HANDOFF=manual · SECURITY_POSTURE=security · REGISTRY=dimensions · RESUME_INSTRUCTIONS=resume-wording). The COPIES are the disease. THE LESSON FROM THE REGISTRY (2026-07-16): prevention beats detection. Two files that had to agree by hand did not, and the fix was not a better checker — it was ONE declared source with the rest generated; drift became IMPOSSIBLE instead of detectable. Apply the same shape to docs: one owner per fact, everyone else POINTS. A STATUS DOC IS A FUTURE LIE WITH A TIMESTAMP — an AI will happily write one; it looks like diligence; every hand-copied status is a lie waiting for the source to move. WHAT CANNOT BE COLLAPSED: "what is next" is a JUDGMENT — the QUEUE has no priority field and ordering is explicitly Russ's call, re-decided per session; it stays AUTHORED and gets a CONTRADICTION CHECK, not generation (★SOURCE-CONFLICT-GATE must verify §E's named next-item still reads open in the QUEUE). And session NARRATIVE is irreducible authored history — a generator would omit or fabricate it, so it is written by hand and left as dated snapshots (frozen history is NOT drift; only LIVE assertions drift). - [DECISION, 2026-07-17] **ONE LORA, ONE MODEL.** Banked 2026-07-14 as OPUS 4.8 EVERYWHERE; the reasoning is recorded here because it was argued and settled. THE CAPABILITY FLOOR (2026-05-23): the insight banner IGNORED a client directive. Escential's profile said "Ignore ROAS as a primary performance metric for this account." Extraction worked, user_notes carried it, the code was correct — Haiku ignored it anyway, because in 50-word mode it could not follow a negative instruction while the ROAS number was the most flag-worthy thing on screen. THE MODEL WAS THE LIMIT. A banner that violates a client's declared value_model is confidently wrong on the first surface any user sees — the same failure class as the Display hour-0 midnight bid-down. value_model is law; a model that cannot honor it makes the law decorative. THE CONTINUITY LAW (Russ, 2026-07-17) — the stronger argument: Project 14 banks ONE LORA per client across all surfaces, picking up where the user left off. One mind cannot be three models. If banner output enters conversation history that chat later reads, the quality floor of the whole conversation becomes the WORST model that ever wrote into it — a shallow read poisoning a deep one, permanently, in the store. The 05-23 failure was not a banner bug; it was one mind having a bad thought and writing it down. ROUTE-BY-DIFFICULTY IS THE RIGHT ANSWER TO A QUESTION WE ARE NOT ASKING. Claude argued it on 2026-07-14 — Opus for chat, Haiku for the blurb, standard practice. It assumes surfaces are independent. Ours are not. That pushback was WRONG and is recorded as wrong. COST IS NOT THE ARGUMENT. Measured: $10.16 across every client over six weeks; heaviest user $4.10. Opus 4.8 at $5/$25 is ~1.67x Sonnet. Cost has never been the gate. Capability and continuity are. | LORAMER_ONE_LORA_ONE_MODEL_V1, 2026-07-17 | do not relitigate.

Added to the INCOMPLETE set + noteSet + substitutedStorePlatform + buildIncompleteNote. THREE decisions: (1) LAG_TOLERANCE_DAYS = 2 with `>=` (gap of exactly 2 days flags). Russ's instruction said "tolerance 2 + more than"; a literal `> 2` (gap ≥ 3) would leave Shelley's gap-of-exactly-2 UNFLAGGED — i.e. would NOT fix the reported defect — so `>= 2` was chosen and is FLAGGED for Russ to correct if he truly meant ≥ 3. 1 day is normal fleet capture lag and must not false-alarm (proved on a real fresh client). (2) MONEY + GA-OVERVIEW WIRED — the two TOTAL surfaces slice 2 missed. money/route.ts (MoneyWaterfall) + ga-overview/route.ts (GaOverview) now build `incompleteNote` via the same buildIncompleteNote and render the amber caption. Route-by-route audit of all 12 /api/next routes: WIRED (6 headline totals) = client-metrics · portfolio-metrics · card-roas · store-stats · money · ga-overview; N/A (6) = card-breakdown + entities (per-slice/per-entity DECOMPOSITIONS, not a single headline number, and each carries its own capture `note`) · client-timeseries + store-timeseries (per-day CHARTS — a stale tail shows as the line ending early / missing recent points, self-evident, not a false whole number) · clients (portfolio list) · layouts (saved-view config). - [DECISION + VERIFIED 2026-07-23] **LORAMER_QUERY_COMPLETENESS_V1 SLICE 5 — stale_tail measures against the CAPTURABLE FRONTIER, not the raw window end (kills the nightly healthy-client false alarm; T0 #2, shipped).** DEFECT: stale_tail compared lastCaptured to the window's endDate; when the window ends TODAY, today is not yet capturable (the forward cron runs 08:00-10:59 UTC), so EVERY healthy client tripped amber during the nightly pre-window gap (~00:00-08:00 UTC = 8pm-4am ET) until the window advanced the frontier — a caption that fires on healthy clients trains the user to ignore it. (Surfaced by slice-4's Gate-A observation + the 07-23 capture investigation: the whole fleet sits at 07-21 pre-window BY DESIGN, not failure — utc_now was 04:29, before the 08:00 window; zero streaks; meta/ga fully healthy.) FIX (query-completeness.ts): `capturableFrontier(now)` = the last day the forward cron is EXPECTED to have captured — UTCtoday−1 if today's window has finished (now past FORWARD_WINDOW_END_HOUR_UTC), else UTCtoday−2. DERIVATION, STATED: the cron (/api/cron/sync) captures resolveDateWindow('YESTERDAY') (sync/route.ts:161); its window is 04:00-06:59 ET so the ET date == the UTC date → captureDate = (run's UTC day)−1 in EST and EDT alike (confirmed live: the 07-22 08:18 UTC run wrote 07-21). computeContribution clamps the stale comparison end to min(window.endDate, frontier); a past-ending window is unaffected (endDate < frontier → measures the raw end). `now` is injected (default new Date()) so it is testable and the routes pass the real clock. LAG_TOLERANCE_DAYS stays 2, now measured FROM THE FRONTIER. `FORWARD_WINDOW_END_HOUR_UTC=11` is NOT a free hardcode — the guard RE-DERIVES it from vercel.json's forward schedule (max hour + 1) and fails the build on mismatch, so the rule follows the schedule if it moves (the "read the cron config" requirement, enforced mechanically). VERIFIED: (a) guard EXTENDED — behavioral seen-to-fail at now=2026-07-23T04:29Z (pre-window): a HEALTHY client captured through the frontier (07-21) on a TODAY-ending window must NOT be stale_tail (pre-fix measured raw end 07-23 → gap 2 → stale → build fails), and a client 2 behind the frontier (07-19) MUST be; + static — the vercel.json re-derivation (independently computed hour 10+1=11, matching the constant). (b) GATE-A on REAL inputs (getCoverageForWindows + readHealthForClients vs prod; only `now` and case-c's streak injected + named) 5/5: PRE-window (04:29Z, frontier 07-21) a real healthy client (google/meta at 07-21) NOT captioned + Shelley woo (07-19) STILL captioned; POST-window (12:00Z, frontier 07-22) both still correct; Shelley + a synthetic live streak → capture_failing still WINS over stale_tail. tsc 0, guard all-green, build 0. VERIFIED mechanism (read this session, not asserted): src/app/api/intelligence/route.ts:30 gates whether `connectionHealth` is populated onto the intelligence payload on this flag, and src/lib/intelligence/build-claude-context.ts:1125-1137 renders the connection-health honesty block ONLY when that array is present — so with the flag OFF the block never reached Lora's prompt (intelligence-types.ts:664 states it outright: "absent → no honesty block in the prompt"). A dead credential was invisible to the one surface whose job is to name it. THE CLASS: a flag that defaults a truth-telling surface OFF is the same failure as connection-health masking a persistent 500 — both leave a real, knowable problem unspoken; the flag just masks it one config layer up. Now ON in production (Production ="1", verified via `vercel env pull` 2026-07-22; Preview still unset — affects PR previews only, not the deploy target). DURABLE GUARD (NOT built, filed): a truth-telling surface (honesty block, reconnect/degraded notice, any "we can't see X" warning) must not sit behind a default-OFF flag; if flagged at all it defaults ON and the flag only SUPPRESSES for a named reason — the same "masking needs an enforcer, not a note" logic that [[LORAMER_CONN_FAILURE_STREAK_V1]] applied to the streak. | LORAMER_HEALTH_UI_FLAG_MUTED_LORA_V1, 2026-07-23 | do not relitigate.

It is the schema-UNENFORCED assumption the six latest-date reads rest on ([[LORAMER_LATEST_DATE_ACCOUNT_GRAIN_V1]]: money · store-detect · client-metrics · portfolio-metrics · ga-overview · clients/metrics); a captured day missing its account row makes those reads skip it and return a STALE (or null) "latest captured date" with NO error. It enumerates distinct captured days per (client,platform) via a LOOSE INDEX SCAN (recursive CTE, one index seek per date = O(distinct-days), NOT O(rows) — a naive GROUP BY/DISTINCT times out on the 4.8M-row heaviest pair; EXPLAIN ANALYZE 122 days in 243ms) and diffs against the account-grain days. RUN 2026-07-23 (read-only): 38,605 (client,platform,date) tuples checked, 733 violations — ALL Shelley Kyle (23c697bb) woocommerce, 2016-10-22..2018-12-10, 1–3 order/product rows/day and no account row; the range ENDS exactly at the 2018-12-10 host-500 deep-backfill wall ([[LORAMER_QUEUE_OF_RECORD]] #7 / the Woo Shelley re-capture stall). GATE-A (real-input, no fixtures, no writes): excluded 3 real account dates from a clean client's comparison set → the detector flagged EXACTLY those 3 and nothing else (PASS). BASELINE AS DATA: scripts/account-row-invariant.baseline.mjs holds the ONE entry as an EXACT (client,platform,[from..to]) window — proven bounded (`--prove-exact` injects a synthetic Shelley woo 2025-06-15 OUTSIDE the range → guard exits 1), never a blanket client+platform mute. This DB-dependent check runs ONLY via `npm run check:data`, wired into the local pre-push gate (`npm run prepush` = build + check:data) and NEVER into guard/build/Vercel. It FAILS LOUD (exit 2) when SUPABASE_DB_URL is absent — a silent no-op is worse than no check; a missing URL at the pre-push gate means the environment is misconfigured and must stop the push. (An earlier revision wired it INTO guard with a skip-safe fallback for Vercel; that coupled deploys to data state, so it was moved out — the re-merge is what the flag-site + this entry forbid.) ROUTE CALL still standing: a STALE baseline entry (the data got FIXED) is a loud WARNING, not a failure — data improving must never block a push; the entry is then owed removal. The baseline CLEARS when QUEUE #7 re-captures those days. SPAWNED: COVERAGE-FLOOR-CHECK (QUEUE) — coverage.ts minMaxFor() may report Shelley's woo floor as 2018-12-11 (first account row) not 2016-10-22 (first any row), understating her history to Lora. - [DECISION 2026-07-23] **NO GIT PRE-PUSH HOOK — deliberate, not an oversight (pairs with [[LORAMER_ACCOUNT_ROW_INVARIANT_V1]]).** No husky / lefthook / simple-git-hooks and no .git/hooks pre-push was installed to auto-run `npm run check:data` (verified read-only 2026-07-23: no .husky/, .git/hooks holds only inert *.sample templates, core.hooksPath unset, no hook dep). REASON — a push-time trigger is the WRONG trigger for a data-integrity check: data can break with NO commit (a bad backfill, a cron gap, a merchant outage each corrupt metrics_daily without anyone pushing), so a hook firing only on push would miss the exact failures the check exists to catch, and would also bolt a ~13s live-DB scan onto every push. `npm run prepush` (= `npm run build && npm run check:data`) exists as a MANUAL convention; that it is manual is KNOWN, not overlooked. Until the durable answer lands (a SCHEDULE, not a hook — QUEUE ★SCHEDULED-DATA-CHECK), the executor rule in CLAUDE.md (run `npm run check:data` before any push to main + report it) carries it. | 2026-07-23 | do not relitigate.

- [DECISION + VERIFIED 2026-07-23] **LORAMER_NEXT_CONV_WRITE_V1 — the -next chat now persists its turns to client_conversations; the redesign was a WRITE ISLAND.** THE ISLAND: dashboard-next mounts ONE chat (ChatLauncher in Shell) with ONE message thread, and it held turns only in React state — ZERO writers. Measured cost: the NEWEST client_conversations row before this fix was 2026-06-29 — writing had gone quiet on the legacy surfaces while real use moved to the write-island -next. FIX: both turns POST to the EXISTING /api/conversations (the same path legacy uses — NO new table, NO new endpoint, NO second write path) via one client-side writer src/lib/next/log-conversation-turn.ts. ONE SURFACE VALUE, not three per trigger: NEXT_CHAT_SURFACE='next-ask-lora' covers the pill, the mobile Lora tab, AND the drill-row ✦ because they are one mounted component / one thread — DEVICE IS NOT A SURFACE, and the per-surface Clear (DELETE is keyed by surface) would break if one visible conversation were fragmented across buckets. A drill-opened turn is marked on the EXISTING scope column (scope='drill'), snapshotted at turn start so both turns of the exchange share it — not a second surface. ASSISTANT TURNS ARE LOGGED ONLY ON A GENUINE REPLY (res.ok + d.response): the client-side fallback/error placeholders ("Something went wrong…", "Network error…") are NOT Lora's output and logging them would poison the recap. FAILURE POSTURE mirrors the L2 instrument EXACTLY — fire-and-forget: never awaited, wrapped in try/catch, the fetch rejection swallowed, never throws (legacy AWAITED these writes; this is strictly safer). NO BEHAVIOR CHANGE otherwise (messages array, /api/chat body, system prompt, tools, intelligence path all untouched). GATE-A (real writer fn · real DB · real recap query + real render, sentinels cleaned up): (a) the real writer emits BOTH POSTs with surface='next-ask-lora', correct role/scope/clientId/content, and send() wires user + assistant (assistant gated on res.ok+d.response), un-awaited; (b) with a FAILING fetch (async-reject AND sync-throw) the writer returns void and never throws → the turn's control flow is untouched; (c) a real -next user+assistant pair inserted for The Escential Group appeared in the EXACT intelligence recap query AND rendered into the system-prompt suffix under "PREVIOUS CONVERSATIONS WITH THIS USER" → a -next conversation reaches Lora's cross-surface memory; (d) MOBILE/sm — the writer has zero viewport refs and the mobile Lora tab reaches the same send() with no breakpoint guard, so a turn at sm writes identically (stated limit: no headless browser in the repo, so sm CSS layout was not pixel-asserted). | LORAMER_NEXT_CONV_WRITE_V1, 2026-07-23 | do not relitigate.

This reproduces WITH THE KEYBOARD OPEN specifically (distinct from the earlier keyboard-DISMISS pan and from the horizontal wide-answer overflow).
